# API examples

> GENERATED by `pnpm openapi` from `src/contract` (every example is validated against the schemas). Do not edit by hand.

Scenario: Monday 2026-10-19. Agenbot loads the day at 07:00 with options A and B; I choose A at 07:42,
mark Martín's call done, move reading; I move Laura's call in Google, Agenbot re-sends the day → proposal → I accept.

Errors common to every route (400 `validation_error`, 401, 403, 409 `idempotency_in_progress`, 422 `idempotency_key_reuse`, 429 `rate_limited`) are listed in `openapi.yaml`.

## Contents

- **Days**: `POST /api/days/{date}`, `GET /api/days/{date}`, `PUT /api/days/{date}`, `PATCH /api/days/{date}`, `POST /api/days/{date}/choose`, `POST /api/days/{date}/shift`, `GET /api/days`
- **Proposals**: `GET /api/days/{date}/proposals`, `POST /api/days/{date}/proposals/{id}/accept`, `POST /api/days/{date}/proposals/{id}/reject`
- **Blocks**: `POST /api/days/{date}/blocks`, `PATCH /api/days/{date}/blocks/{id}`, `PATCH /api/days/{date}/blocks/by-external/{externalId}`, `DELETE /api/days/{date}/blocks/{id}`, `POST /api/days/{date}/blocks/reorder`
- **Habits**: `GET /api/habits`, `POST /api/habits`, `PATCH /api/habits/{habit}`, `GET /api/habits/{habit}/logs`, `POST /api/habits/{habit}/logs`
- **Tasks**: `GET /api/tasks`, `POST /api/tasks`, `PATCH /api/tasks/{id}`, `DELETE /api/tasks/{id}`
- **Metrics**: `GET /api/metrics`
- **Agent**: `GET /api/agent/context`, `GET /api/changelog`, `GET /api/webhook/status`
- **Sleep**: `GET /api/sleep`, `POST /api/sleep/events`, `PUT /api/sleep/{date}`
- **Ops**: `GET /api/health`, `GET /api/openapi.json`, `GET /api/export`, `POST /api/internal/close-day`
- **Auth**: `GET /api/auth/session`, `POST /api/auth/setup`, `POST /api/auth/unlock`, `POST /api/auth/recover`, `POST /api/auth/pin`, `POST /api/auth/recovery-codes`, `POST /api/auth/passkey/register/options`, `POST /api/auth/passkey/register/verify`, `POST /api/auth/passkey/auth/options`, `POST /api/auth/passkey/auth/verify`, `DELETE /api/auth/passkey`, `GET /api/auth/devices`, `PATCH /api/auth/devices/{id}`, `DELETE /api/auth/devices/{id}`, `POST /api/auth/logout`
- **Push**: `POST /api/push/subscribe`, `DELETE /api/push/subscribe`
- **Webhook**: `POST ${AGENT_WEBHOOK_URL}`

## Days

### `POST /api/days/{date}` — "Armar mi día": create my own day (user)

Access: **user**

Creates the day with one empty option `A` ("Mi día"), already `chosen`, so I can add blocks before Agenbot loads it. A later agent `PUT` becomes a proposal. 409 if the day exists.

#### 06:40 — Agenbot no cargó todavía; armo mi día
_user_

```http
POST /api/days/2026-10-20
Cookie: agenda_session=<session>
Content-Type: application/json

{}
```

<details><summary>Response 201</summary>

### `GET /api/days/{date}` — Get a full day

Access: **agent+user**

Options with blocks, tasks, habit logs and the pending proposal. Reading a past day that is not `closed` closes it first (lazy close).

#### 07:01 — draft with options A and B
_user_

```http
GET /api/days/2026-10-19
Cookie: agenda_session=<session>
```

### `PUT /api/days/{date}` — Create or replace the whole day (agent)

Access: **agent**

Idempotent full replace. Calendar blocks are matched by `externalId`, agent blocks by `id`.
- No day → **201** `created` (status `draft`).
- `draft` → **200** `replaced` (my tracking fields are preserved on matched blocks).
- `chosen | edited` → **202** `proposed`: nothing changes, a `Proposal` is created (superseding any pending one) and I get a push.
- `?mode=force` → applied immediately with the same preservation rules (my tracking fields, `userOverrides`, `locked` blocks); logged as forced.
- `closed` → **409** `day_closed`.
Calendar blocks missing from the body are deleted, unless I already tracked them: then they are kept with `removedFromCalendarAt`.
On `chosen | edited` days the body must include the chosen option's label (else 422 `validation_error`); options it omits are kept unchanged.

### `PATCH /api/days/{date}` — Update day fields

Access: **agent+user**

`notes`, `callsSummary`, `weeklyGym`, `review`; `status: closed` (user only) closes the day early.

#### 22:40 — I fill the end-of-day review
_user_

```http
PATCH /api/days/2026-10-19
Cookie: agenda_session=<session>
If-Match: "5"
Idempotency-Key: 019a0a6e-ffff-7000-8000-000000000001
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "review": {
    "energy": 4,
    "mood": 4,
    "text": "Buen día. Martín pide hablar con la pareja; Laura cerró follow-up."
  }
}
```

### `POST /api/days/{date}/choose` — Choose a plan option

Access: **agent+user**

Allowed while `draft` or `chosen` (switching). `edited` / `closed` → 409. Sends the webhook immediately.

#### Switching option after editing
_user_

```http
POST /api/days/2026-10-19/choose
Cookie: agenda_session=<session>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "option": "B"
}
```

```http
HTTP/1.1 409 Conflict
Content-Type: application/problem+json

{
  "type": "https://agenda.lemydev.com/problems/invalid_transition",
  "title": "Invalid status transition",
  "status": 409,
  "detail": "Cannot switch option on an edited day.",
  "code": "invalid_transition",
  "requestId": "req_01JA9X7Q2M8K"
}
```

### `POST /api/days/{date}/shift` — "Me atrasé": shift my pending blocks

Access: **agent+user**

Moves pending, unlocked `agent`/`user` blocks of the chosen option starting at or after `fromTime`, `sleep` included; calendar blocks never move. Blocks can pass midnight (`24:15` = 00:15 next day). When I shift, `start`/`end` join `userOverrides`. Recorded as a `shift` ChangeLog entry and a `shift` webhook change.

#### 11:04 — +30 min from 11:00
_user_

```http
POST /api/days/2026-10-19/shift
Cookie: agenda_session=<session>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "fromTime": "11:00",
  "minutes": 30
}
```

### `POST /api/days/{date}/proposals/{id}/accept` — Accept a proposal (user)

Access: **user**

Applies the payload: blocks matched by `externalId`, then `id`; my tracking fields, `userOverrides` and `locked` blocks are preserved. Webhook sent immediately.

#### 12:41 — I accept
_user_

```http
POST /api/days/2026-10-19/proposals/019a0a6e-1c00-7000-8000-0000000001f4/accept
Cookie: agenda_session=<session>
```

## Blocks

### `POST /api/days/{date}/blocks` — Add a block

Access: **agent+user**

Added to the chosen option, or to `?option=` (required while `draft`). A user block makes a `chosen` day `edited`; all its schedule fields are mine (`userOverrides`). Only the agent may add `calendar` blocks.

### `PATCH /api/days/{date}/blocks/{id}` — Edit a block

Access: **agent+user**

Ownership rules:
- Calendar blocks: schedule fields → 409 `calendar_owned` for me.
- Agent/user blocks: each schedule field I edit joins `userOverrides`. The agent writing one of those, or anything on a `locked` block → 409 `user_edit_conflict` (agent `force: true` overrides, except `locked`).
- Tracking fields (`status`, `outcome`, `note`) are mine; the agent may set them only while unset / `pending`.

#### 10:48 — Martín's call done, outcome follow_up
_user_

```http
PATCH /api/days/2026-10-19/blocks/019a0a6e-1c00-7000-8000-000000000064
Cookie: agenda_session=<session>
If-Match: "1"
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "status": "done",
  "outcome": "follow_up",
  "note": "Le interesa, pide hablar con la pareja. Volver a llamar el jueves 18:00."
}
```

```http
HTTP/1.1 200 OK
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "id": "019a0a6e-1c00-7000-8000-000000000064",
```

### `DELETE /api/days/{date}/blocks/{id}` — Delete a block

Access: **agent+user**

Calendar blocks → 409 `calendar_owned` for me. The agent cannot delete my blocks or blocks I tracked (`user_edit_conflict`).

### `POST /api/days/{date}/blocks/reorder` — Reorder blocks of one option

Access: **agent+user**

Sets `order` (tie-breaker among blocks with the same start). Moving in time is a `PATCH` of `start`.

### `POST /api/sleep/events` — "Me voy a dormir" / "Me levanté"

Access: **agent+user**

Sets `bedAt` or `wokeAt` (now, or `at`). Night = previous day if the time is before 12:00. `bed` also marks the pending `sleep` block of that day done. The agent can only fill an empty half.

#### 00:20 — I tap "Me voy a dormir" (night of the 19th)
_user_

```http
POST /api/sleep/events
Cookie: agenda_session=<session>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "type": "bed"
}
```

```http
Content-Type: application/json

  "wakeSource": null,
  "durationMinutes": null,

  "plannedEnd": "07:00",
  "bedDelayMinutes": 50,
```

### `GET /api/openapi.json` — This OpenAPI document

Access: **public**

### `POST /api/internal/close-day` — Close past days (Coolify scheduled task, 00:05 ART)

Access: **internal**

Closes every unclosed day before today and purges idempotency keys older than 48 h. Idempotent.

#### 00:05 on 2026-10-20
_internal_

```http
POST /api/internal/close-day
X-Internal-Token: <INTERNAL_TOKEN>
Content-Type: application/json

{}
```

```http
HTTP/1.1 200 OK
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "closed": [
    "2026-10-19"
  ],
  "purgedIdempotencyKeys": 14
}
```

### `POST /api/auth/unlock` — Unlock / log in with PIN

Access: **preauth**

Trusts a new device or re-unlocks a locked one. 5 failures → locked 15 min, doubling per lockout (cap 24 h), per device and globally.

#### Correct PIN
_anonymous_

```http
POST /api/auth/unlock
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "pin": "482913"
}
```

```http
HTTP/1.1 200 OK
Content-Type: application/json
Set-Cookie: agenda_session=<token>; Path=/; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax; Max-Age=7776000

{
  "device": {
    "id": "019a0a6e-1c00-7000-8000-000000000320",
    "name": "Galaxy S26 Ultra · Chrome",
    "userAgent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Linux; Android 16; SM-S948B) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/141.0 Mobile Safari/537.36",
    "current": true,
    "passkeyEnabled": true,
    "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:12:00.000-03:00",
    "lastSeenAt": "2026-10-19T07:41:30.000-03:00",
    "lastUnlockAt": "2026-10-19T07:41:30.000-03:00"
  }
}
```

#### Wrong PIN
_anonymous_

```http
POST /api/auth/unlock
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "pin": "111111"
}
```

```http
HTTP/1.1 401 Unauthorized
Content-Type: application/problem+json

{
  "type": "https://agenda.lemydev.com/problems/invalid_credentials",
  "title": "Invalid credentials",
  "status": 401,
  "detail": "Wrong PIN.",
  "code": "invalid_credentials",
  "requestId": "req_01JA9X7Q2M8K",
  "remainingAttempts": 3
}
```

#### 5th failure
_anonymous_

```http
POST /api/auth/unlock
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "pin": "222222"
}
```

```http
HTTP/1.1 429 Too Many Requests
Content-Type: application/problem+json
Retry-After: 900

{
  "type": "https://agenda.lemydev.com/problems/locked_out",
  "title": "Too many failed attempts",
  "status": 429,
  "detail": "Try again in 15 minutes.",
  "code": "locked_out",
  "requestId": "req_01JA9X7Q2M8K",
  "retryAfterSeconds": 900
}
```

```http
  "event": "day.updated_by_user",
```
